# Skills · C · part 4

889 entries, cadenza-spec-lint to calculate-williams-percent-r. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cadenza-spec-lint**: Lint Cadenza spec files and surface structural findings. Use this when the user invokes cadenza-spec-lint, asks to validate spec structure, edits spec/**/*.md, or needs the spec:lint completion gate. ([cadenza-dev/cadenza](https://github.com/cadenza-dev/cadenza/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cadenza-spec-lint) · ★4)
- **cadenza-wizard**: Prepare Cadenza Wizard handoffs at workflow boundaries. Use this skill whenever the user asks for Wizard work, a next Architect kick file, phase-boundary handoff, Phase 0 bootstrap from Scout to Architect, Scout-to-Architect planning, or any Cadenza handoff that must summarize accepted inputs without changing specs, production code, or phase pointers. ([cadenza-dev/cadenza](https://github.com/cadenza-dev/cadenza/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cadenza-wizard) · ★4)
- **caderno-didatico**: Use ao criar ou revisar aulas, capítulos, notas de estudo e PDFs ilustrados deste projeto. O padrão local é editorial limpo, com título centralizado só na abertura, preferindo uma linha; todo termo destacado em amarelo deve ter cartão explicativo na mesma página, sem setas. Use desenhos originais detalhados com fundo bege, sem números nem chamadas numeradas no texto. Organize os blocos pelo espaço real, evitando grandes vazios internos. Use linguagem acessível e explicação progressiva. Caderno pautado e colorido somente quando solicitado. ([Uri-Wernik/cyber-learning](https://github.com/Uri-Wernik/cyber-learning/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/caderno-didatico) · ★2)

A utiliser des qu'un travail depasse quelques heures, qu'on… ([MasterLaplace/LplCraftSkills](https://github.com/MasterLaplace/LplCraftSkills/tree/HEAD/skills/cadrer-et-planifier))
- **cadrer-story**: Transforme un requis brut — une phrase, une décision consignée, un finding d'audit, une contrainte reçue d'un tiers — en stories `à faire` prêtes pour /deliver-story : critères en Étant donné/Quand/Alors, décisions consignées, priorité et dépendances posées. Écrit dans un fichier d'epic, ne code jamais. ([jamyl/socle](https://github.com/jamyl/socle/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cadrer-story) · ★1)
- **cadrer-un-projet**: Use when the user describes something to build that has no ticket and no code yet — a new project, or a feature they state before it exists. Runs three framing stages in conversation and lands the answers in the project sheet, never in a standalone document. Do not use for a question about existing code, for a ticket that already exists, or for a "what if we…" that is not going to be built. ([AxiaCoder/claude-config](https://github.com/AxiaCoder/claude-config/tree/HEAD/skills/cadrer-un-projet))
- **cadrer-un-visuel**: Use when a screen or interface is about to be built and no mockup is referenced — in the ticket or in the project sheet — or when the user asks to mock up, sketch or think through the design of such a screen. Load it BEFORE any mockup tool: it frames the screen in conversation, checks the user's design choices against a short set of UX principles and says so when one is off, settles where the mockup lives, and only then hands the drawing to the mockup tool. Typically raised by `cadrer-un-projet` or `start-ticket`, but also reached directly. Do not use for a pure back-end change, for a fix to… ([AxiaCoder/claude-config](https://github.com/AxiaCoder/claude-config/tree/HEAD/skills/cadrer-un-visuel))
- **cads-whole-body-ct-eval**: Evaluates AI models on voxel-level segmentation of 167 whole-body anatomical structures from CT scans. It probes generalization across diverse imaging protocols, patient demographics, and pathological conditions, with a focus on clinical utility in radiation oncology. Use when the user wants to benchmark on CADS-dataset, 18 Public Benchmark Datasets, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Dice coefficient. - **cady**: Reads the company's context in Cady, then loads the matching skill. Use when the work is a company's sales and outreach, drafting to a person (read the sender's voice pack first), posts, competitors and pricing, tools and integrations, budget, fundraising, filings and hiring, product and engineering work, and company agents. Not for personal tasks or coding with no company context. Requires a Cady account. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/cady/skills/cady))
- **cady-accounting-method**: Accounting role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Accounting agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-accounting-method))
- **cady-accounting-period-close**: Month-end and period close. Use when: Close the books for a month, quarter or year: reconcile bank, payouts and revenue, clear Suspense and lock. Not for: Preparing a tax return (use `cady-finance-tax-cycle`), budgets or runway, or audit sign-off. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-accounting-period-close))
- **cady-company-change-agent-behaviour**: Change agent behaviour. Use when: Change what agents do: edit a skill, role card, run sequence, tool text or an agent's goal. Not for: Making, scheduling or retiring an agent (`cady-company-manage-agents`), or writing or judging a post or design. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-change-agent-behaviour))
- **cady-company-decision**: Who decides, and how to record it. Use when: Before a choice or an effect: whether it is decided, who decides, how to test a big call and how to record it. Not for: Gathering the evidence a call needs (`cady-company-evidence`), the weekly goal calls (`cady-company-weekly-review`), or doing the task itself. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-decision))
- **cady-company-evidence**: Key questions and behaviour tests. Use when: A decision you own needs evidence: frame its questions, take the cheapest route, pre-register any test of behaviour. Not for: Dashboards, reports or data no decision needs; company goals and strategy (`cady-company-weekly-review`); deciding the answer for another owner; routine delivery whose desired behaviour is clear (`cady-engineering-method`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-evidence))
- **cady-company-manage-agents**: Manage company agents. Use when: Create and bind, schedule, review or retire a company agent: whether one is warranted, its design and first task. Not for: Native helpers, subagents, one-off work items and agents on a host that is in contact. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-manage-agents))
- **cady-company-method**: Executive role card: its point and key bets. Load when acting as the Executive agent (the outer loop). Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-method))
- **cady-company-onboarding**: Set up a company and hand over to its Executive. Use when: A new company has no Cady setup yet: record it from what the owner says and set up its Executive agent. Not for: Designing or taking an agent live (`cady-company-manage-agents`), or building a first offering (`cady-gtm-launch`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-onboarding))
- **cady-company-operations-compliance-register**: Compliance register and monthly filing review. Use when: What statutory and registry filings the company owes and when (annual returns, confirmation statements, state reports). Not for: Preparing one tax return (`cady-finance-tax-cycle`), submitting any filing or payment, or legal advice. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-compliance-register))
- **cady-company-operations-contract-checks**: Contract checks: before signing, after completion. Use when: A contract that commits the company is about to be signed or was just signed: what to settle first and what it starts. Not for: Posts or updates about it, the yearly filing calendar (`cady-company-operations-compliance-register`), general legal questions, or a contract not yet in view. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-contract-checks))
- **cady-company-operations-inbox-follow-through**: Inbox follow-through: group, triage, track commitments. Use when: Bounded inbox maintenance or thread triage for follow-through, marketing handling or commitments needing tracking. Not for: Executing a prospect reply (`cady-gtm-reply`), sending or drafting outreach, or reading mail Cady is not given: only the host's connector reads mail. Thread triage remains in scope. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-inbox-follow-through))
- **cady-company-operations-method**: Company Operations role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Company Operations agent or choosing among its skills. - **cady-company-operations-people-lifecycle**: Joiners, movers and leavers. Use when: Plan, track and verify the paperwork and access for a new hire or contractor, a role change or someone leaving. Not for: Running payroll, buying licences, or employment or immigration legal advice. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-people-lifecycle))
- **cady-company-operations-requirement-register**: Requirement register: research, check and record. Use when: Research, independently check and record one compliance requirement as a Cady Projects decision. Not for: Tracking occurrences or events (the calling skill does that), filing, signing or legal advice. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-operations-requirement-register))
- **cady-company-taste-review**: Writing craft and taste decisions. Use when: Write or edit a post, article, web copy or other public text, or review a design (judge with the taste check). Not for: Pass/fail checks a script or test can decide (run those first), a direct message to one person, or writing a prompt or skill and changing how agents behave (`cady-company-change-agent-behaviour`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-taste-review))
- **cady-company-weekly-review**: Weekly review. Use when: The weekly review: assess the company, call every goal and initiative, check bets, hand changes to the orchestrator. Not for: A new company's setup (`cady-company-onboarding`), pushing work along, scheduling or dispatching agents or doing function work. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-company-weekly-review))
- **cady-customer-success-lifecycle**: Customer lifecycle: first value to renewal. Use when: Run one customer account after the sale: onboarding to first value, health, risk, renewal, expansion or cancellation. Not for: Open deals before signature (`cady-sales-deal-motion`), the company's own vendor renewals (`cady-finance-vendor-spend-review`), or message copy alone (`cady-gtm-reply` or `cady-gtm-warm-email`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-customer-success-lifecycle))
- **cady-customer-success-method**: Customer Success role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Customer Success agent or choosing among its skills. - **cady-design-method**: Design role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Design agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-design-method))
- **cady-design-wcag-audit**: Accessibility audit (WCAG 2.2 AA). Use when: Audit a web or app interface against WCAG 2.2 AA with real tools and report ranked, reproducible findings. Not for: Visual direction or layout critique (use `cady-design-method`), functional journey QA (use `cady-qa-method`) or a legal conformance statement (needs a qualified specialist). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-design-wcag-audit))
- **cady-engineering-debugging**: Debugging and incident response. Use when: Find and fix the root cause of a bug, failing check or production incident, then the regression test and postmortem. Not for: Rating a bug's severity or triaging a flaky test, reviewing someone's diff (use `cady-engineering-independent-review`) or recurring backlog review (use `cady-engineering-system-cycle`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-debugging))
- **cady-engineering-independent-review**: Independent review. Independently review a diff, artifact or decision record: rerun its checks, rank findings and return land, fix or redesign. Use when: a change needs review, before merge or in a later batch, or a decision others will build on before it is acted on. Not for: root-causing a bug, triaging a bug report or running helpers (`cady-orchestrator-goal-pass`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-independent-review))
- **cady-engineering-integrations**: Integrations. Choose, operate or build a company integration: compare and pick a tool or vendor to connect (the vendor reference), operate a chosen connector (the connector reference) or build one (the build reference). Use when: comparing, choosing or connecting a tool, data source or account. Not for: a purchase or renewal decision on cost (use `cady-finance-vendor-spend-review`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-integrations))
- **cady-engineering-method**: Engineering role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Engineering agent or choosing among its skills. - **cady-engineering-system-cycle**: Recurring system cycle: map, dimensions, routed motions. Use when: Clear a product's engineering backlog in recurring cycles from its goal map, system dimensions and state-routed motions. Not for: One bounded change with clear acceptance (use `cady-engineering-method`) or deciding what to build and for whom (use `cady-product-manage`). ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-engineering-system-cycle))
- **cady-finance-budget-review**: Budget and cost review. Use when: Draft or update a budget or spend allocation, or review last month's spend against it. - **cady-finance-method**: Finance role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Finance agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-finance-method))
- **cady-finance-runway**: Burn and runway. Use when: Work out burn, runway months and default alive or dead from actual cash, then decide when to cut and what first. Not for: Drafting a budget or allocation (use `cady-finance-budget-review`), deciding a raise (use `cady-fundraise-raise-plan`), approving spend or moving money. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-finance-runway))
- **cady-finance-tax-cycle**: Entity tax and filing cycle. Use when: Plan or review an entity's tax obligations, filing preparation and material strategy changes. Not for: Filing, payment, legal advice, invented jurisdiction rules or a universal daily tax agent; use `cady-accounting-method` for period close. - **cady-finance-vendor-spend-review**: Vendor spend review: new purchase or renewal. Not for: Bookkeeping, a finance ledger, payment, provider billing changes, supplier order execution or technical qualification; use `cady-finance-budget-review` for the current budget plan. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-finance-vendor-spend-review))
- **cady-fundraise-investor-list**: Ranked investor list. Use when: Decide who to pitch for a round: a ranked list of named investors and partners, sourced, with warm paths. - **cady-fundraise-investor-outreach**: Investor outreach and updates. Use when: Write to investors: a warm intro request, a personalised first note, round follow-ups, or the monthly update send. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-fundraise-investor-outreach))
- **cady-fundraise-method**: Fundraise role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the Fundraise agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-fundraise-method))
- **cady-fundraise-raise-materials**: Raise materials, data room and investor updates. Use when: Draft or revise an investor memo, deck narrative, data room or monthly investor update for a founder. Not for: Deciding amount, instrument or terms (use `cady-fundraise-raise-plan`), choosing which investors to pitch, sending, or editing the cap table. - **cady-fundraise-raise-plan**: Raise plan: amount, instrument, dilution and process. Not for: Building the investor list, drafting the deck, memo or update, writing to investors, or legal and tax advice. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-fundraise-raise-plan))
- **cady-gtm-calls**: Sales calls: prepare and follow up. Use when: Prepare for a sales or customer call (account brief, agenda, questions), then record its follow-up and next step. Not for: Dialling, recording audio, cold email, or an unverified call note; use `cady-gtm-warm-email` for a separately authorized message. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-calls))
- **cady-gtm-channel-plan**: Channel plan. Use when: Turn accepted persona, offer and chosen channels into channel plays and execution briefs. Not for: Choosing the persona, offer or channel mix; researching parties, writing copy, sending or installing schedules. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-channel-plan))
- **cady-gtm-cold-email-cycle**: Cold email cycle. Use when: Write and send cold email to people not yet contacted, using one template per segment. Not for: Engaged parties, partner or organiser business approaches, replies in a live thread, and every follow-up; use `cady-gtm-warm-email` for partner approaches and follow-ups, or `cady-gtm-reply` for inbound replies. Missing verification holds release, not preparation. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-cold-email-cycle))
- **cady-gtm-events**: Events: attend or host. Use when: Prepare for or host the event named in the brief to advance the company through real conversations. Not for: Choosing an event or treating an attendee directory as intent. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-events))
- **cady-gtm-launch**: GTM launch session. Prepare a coordinated go-to-market launch from strategy through approved work. Use when: the company is starting a growth motion. Not for: choosing a persona (use `cady-gtm-offer-and-segments`) or drafting outreach. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-launch))
- **cady-gtm-linkedin-message**: Social direct message. Use when: Draft a first social DM, LinkedIn connection note or one no-reply follow-up. Not for: Public comments or posts are publishing; email or a reply in a live thread use their own skills. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-linkedin-message))
- **cady-gtm-method**: GTM role card: what this function owns and decides. Load when acting as the GTM agent or choosing among its skills. Not for doing a task: load the task skill. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-method))
- **cady-gtm-offer-and-segments**: Offer and persona. Use when: Choose or revise the target persona, offer or message angle from evidence, before a cold cycle and at its review. Not for: Preparing or releasing a message; use `cady-gtm-cold-email-cycle`, `cady-gtm-warm-email`, `cady-gtm-reply` or `cady-gtm-linkedin-message`. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-offer-and-segments))
- **cady-gtm-persona-profile**: Persona profile. Use when: Write or revise one persona's profile (customer, investor, partner or supplier) before positioning, sourcing or copy. Not for: Choosing the offer or promise (`cady-gtm-offer-and-segments`), sourcing a List (`cady-gtm-tam-map`) or preparing a message. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-persona-profile))
- **cady-gtm-pipeline-pass**: Warm pipeline pass. Use when: Pass over due tracks of one motion (customer for GTM, investor for fundraising, partner) and their next steps. Not for: Writing one message (use `cady-gtm-warm-email`, `cady-gtm-reply` or `cady-gtm-linkedin-message`) or working one deal in depth. - **cady-gtm-reply**: Reply in a live thread. Use when: Answer an inbound reply in a live thread: a question, interest, objection, referral, not now or stop. Not for: First touches and follow-ups to someone who has not replied. ([malcolmai/cady-skills](https://github.com/malcolmai/cady-skills/tree/HEAD/library/cady-gtm-reply))
- **cady-gtm-tam-map**: TAM map. Use when: Find who wants, where they gather: people, communities, prospects, leads, target customers, companies or accounts.
